# 实测接口与字段

以下字段来自微信开发者工具抓包和原服务公开 GET 实测响应。接口外层通常为 `code`、`data`、`msg`、`success`。线路资源日期与票种是 2026-10-13 的时点样本，库存和价格会变化。

| 方法 | 路径与查询 | `data` 结构 | 已观察字段 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `POST` | `/base/app/v1/advert/selectAppAdvert` | 对象 | `typeMap` |
| `GET` | `/base/app/v1/advert/selectPopUpAdvert` | 对象 | `code`、`data`、`msg`、`success` |
| `GET` | `/base/app/v1/city/queryCityList` | 数组 | — |
| `GET` | `/base/app/v1/contentApp/queryWaterfallFlowList?contentType=&current=1&size=10` | 对象 | `records`、`total`、`size`、`current`、`orders`、`optimizeCountSql`、`searchCount`、`optimizeJoinOfCountSql` |
| `GET` | `/base/app/v1/contentApp/queryWaterfallFlowList?contentType=&current=2&size=10` | 对象 | `records`、`total`、`size`、`current`、`orders`、`optimizeCountSql`、`searchCount`、`optimizeJoinOfCountSql` |
| `GET` | `/base/app/v1/contentApp/queryWaterfallFlowList?contentType=&current=3&size=10` | 对象 | `records`、`total`、`size`、`current`、`orders`、`optimizeCountSql`、`searchCount`、`maxLimit`、`countId`、`pages` |
| `GET` | `/base/app/v1/contentApp/queryWaterfallFlowList?contentType=&current=4&size=10` | 对象 | `records`、`total`、`size`、`current`、`orders`、`optimizeCountSql`、`searchCount`、`maxLimit`、`countId`、`pages` |
| `GET` | `/base/app/v1/contentRanking/home/ranking?count=3` | 数组 | — |
| `POST` | `/base/app/v1/contentRanking/selectRankingDetails?contentId=1418152026310770690&current=1&size=10` | 对象 | `id`、`contentId`、`rankingName`、`rankingClass`、`rankingType`、`rankingMainPictureUrl`、`rankingDescribe`、`rankingTotalUserData`、`rankingInclude`、`rankingPersonalInfo`、`rankingSharePictureUrl`、`bottomColour`、`tagUrl`、`travelMapFlag`、`type`、`rankingItemList`、`sumCount`、`favoriteSum`、`personalInfoValue` |
| `POST` | `/base/app/v1/contentRanking/selectRankingDetails?contentId=1418152026310770690&current=2&size=10` | 对象 | `id`、`contentId`、`rankingName`、`rankingClass`、`rankingType`、`rankingMainPictureUrl`、`rankingDescribe`、`rankingTotalUserData`、`rankingInclude`、`rankingPersonalInfo`、`rankingSharePictureUrl`、`bottomColour`、`tagUrl`、`travelMapFlag`、`type`、`rankingItemList`、`sumCount`、`favoriteSum`、`personalInfoValue` |
| `POST` | `/base/app/v1/contentRanking/selectRankingDetails?contentId=1418152026344325122&current=1&size=10` | 对象 | `id`、`contentId`、`rankingName`、`rankingClass`、`rankingType`、`rankingMainPictureUrl`、`rankingDescribe`、`rankingTotalUserData`、`rankingInclude`、`rankingPersonalInfo`、`rankingSharePictureUrl`、`bottomColour`、`tagUrl`、`travelMapFlag`、`type`、`rankingItemList`、`sumCount`、`favoriteSum`、`personalInfoValue` |

## 说明

- 查询参数只覆盖已实测组合；日期和库存样本可能过期，后续页码需要继续抓取。
- 登录 code、Cookie、令牌、请求头及原始 HAR 均未存储在项目中。
- 详情页中的记录字段与图片 URL 可在对应 JSON 样本中检查。
